SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/ablation_initial/generic_same_structured_evidence/meta.py characters [0,2788) of 2788
"""CLI/backend adaptation of the HyperAgents self-referential meta entry point."""
def generic_plan(packet):
    # Keep the complete explicit archive available and use the common probe menu.
    scenes = packet['development']
    probes = []
    if scenes and scenes[0]['status'] == 'complete':
        probes = [{'scene_id': scenes[0]['scene_id'], 'kind': kind} for kind in ['camera_focal', 'object_scale']]
    return {'probes': probes, 'regression_guidance': True,
            'instruction': 'Improve the shared task program H and your meta-program M. Inspect the entire supplied development archive, sources, execution errors, renders, geometry, and regression measurements. Design a general change to the reconstruction process under the fixed objective and limits. You may change meta.py planning, probe selection, evidence prioritization, and the next research instruction. Preserve callable plan(packet). The next candidate uses the latest valid meta program; this is an exploration branch, not acceptance of its task program. Do not specialize to scene identifiers.'}

def structured_evidence(packet):
    scenes = packet['development']
    diagnoses = []
    for scene in scenes:
        if scene['status'] != 'complete':
            diagnoses.append({'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'object_id': 'unknown', 'code_location': 'unknown', 'observation': 'No valid output; consult execution log', 'observation_type': 'measured', 'hypothesis': 'unknown', 'probe': 'none', 'probe_result': 'not_run', 'planned_change': 'Investigate general runtime failure before visual diagnosis'})
            continue
        target = scene.get('target_object', 'unknown')
        diagnoses.append({'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'object_id': target, 'code_location': 'unknown', 'observation': 'Reference/render disagreement; magnitude is measured by fixed visual metrics', 'observation_type': 'measured_metric_with_unresolved_visual_cause', 'hypothesis': 'Camera framing or object scale may contribute; this is a hypothesis, not attribution', 'probe': 'camera_focal and object_scale', 'probe_result': 'awaiting controlled intervention', 'planned_change': 'Use probe sensitivity and dependency regression evidence to choose a shared H change'})
        break
    failures = {key: sum((not row.get('functional', {}).get(key, False) for row in scenes)) for key in ['structure_pass', 'material_pass', 'composite_pass']}
    priorities = sorted(failures, key=lambda key: (-failures[key], key))
    return {'diagnoses': diagnoses, 'regression_priorities': priorities}
def plan(packet):
    generic = generic_plan(packet)
    evidence = structured_evidence(packet)
    generic['diagnoses'] = evidence['diagnoses']
    generic['regression_priorities'] = evidence['regression_priorities']
    return generic

SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/backend/harnesses/baseline/policy.py characters [0,1537) of 1537
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re


def solve(task, runtime):
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [{'role': 'system', 'content': prompt}, {'role': 'user', 'content': [
        {'type': 'text', 'text': 'Reconstruct this RGB image. Match its camera and framing. ' +
         f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. Budget: {runtime.remaining()}"},
        runtime.image(task['image'])]}]
    while runtime.remaining()['model_calls'] > 0 and runtime.remaining()['seconds'] > 0:
        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        messages.append(response)
        text = response['content'] or ''
        blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S)
        if not blocks:
            if text.strip().upper() == 'DONE': return
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': 'Return one complete program in a python code block, or DONE.'})
            continue
        if runtime.remaining()['executions'] <= 0: return
        result = runtime.execute(blocks[-1])
        feedback = {'type': 'text', 'text': json.dumps({k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'}) +
                    '\nCompare the rendered camera view with the original reference. ' +
                    f'Fix errors with a complete revised program, or finish with DONE. Remaining: {runtime.remaining()}'}
        content = [feedback]
        if result['image']: content.append(runtime.image(result['image']))
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})

SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/external_followup_inputs/bw_0011/scene_rsi_pilot/policy.py characters [0,12776) of 12776
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re


def _text(response):
    content = response.get('content') or ''
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    if isinstance(content, list):
        return '\n'.join(item.get('text', '') for item in content if isinstance(item, dict))
    return ''


PROCEDURE = '''
Additional shared procedure:
Build reliability comes before visual refinement. The fixed execution budget includes
construction, realization and output, not just renderer sampling. Leave all runner-owned
render settings unchanged: do not set engine, samples, denoising, resolution, output paths
or time limits. Camera, lights, world and scoped materials remain available as documented.

Make the FIRST program a complete economical scene, with all major visible semantic
objects, their attached parts, support surfaces and foreground crops. Economize repeated
surface decoration before simplifying major silhouettes or deleting inventory. Use a small
reusable palette instead of assigning a distinct random finish to every repeated element.
A straight tube needs one rod; do not densely sample straight stretches. In this fixed
implementation sweep_tube with n points expands to 2*n-1 primitive meshes, and a fillet_box
can expand to 23 primitive meshes. Assemblies and clones do not merge their meshes.
Count expanded primitives, not builder calls. A few hundred primitive meshes is an initial
planning heuristic, not an acceptance threshold or a measured safe runtime. Spend extra
geometry on visible curvature and negative space only when its image contribution warrants it.
Do not alter SceneAct, merge semantic owners, or rename groups to influence target selection.

Include a concise self-review in the complete program's comments before submitting it:
- inventory, owner, actual support surface, camera anchors and uncertainty;
- repeated-loop expansion and the most expensive decorative families;
- positive finite dimensions, valid fillet radii, distinct rod endpoints, unique include slots;
- realize before camera/world/lights, and nonempty correctly scoped material descendants.
This is a code review, not an execution or a claim of passing regression tests. Do not spend
a separate model turn merely declaring READY unless explicitly asked for such a review.

Separate camera, each semantic object's dimensions/pose, and appearance parameters.
Before changing camera, compare at least three independent visible anchors spanning depth
and image position: architectural edge directions, a near crop/contact, and a far object or
wall feature. Record approximate reference/render locations and mismatch directions in
comments, marking them as visual estimates. An unchanged anchor between two generated
renders is not evidence that it matches the reference. Prefer a modest correction supported
by several anchors. Do not rescale all objects to compensate for an uncertain camera.
Review scale at the complete semantic owner, not an automatically selected trim or child.

Review broad appearance alongside geometry: light/dark region ordering, dominant surface
colors, shadow locations and contrast. A consistent appearance mismatch across several
surfaces may justify one lighting/world correction with geometry and camera fixed. A local
mismatch may justify a scoped material correction with lighting fixed. Do not change both
indiscriminately. Preserve non-target shaders, mesh data and transforms during local edits.
Never infer a material's physical color uniquely from its photographed brightness.

For missing objects, trace builder calls, attached returned nodes and unique include slots,
then compare camera projection, actual wall/support faces and nearer occluders. Construction
is not proof of visibility. Check the object's full extent against perpendicular wall returns
as well as its nominal support plane before translating it. Preserve reference occluders;
do not merely move an item until it is exposed. Keep all dependent parts together and
recompute actual contact offsets after size or pose changes. AABB tops and overlaps alone
cannot certify contact or collision. Unknown ownership/name mappings remain unknown.

After a valid render, choose the largest supported discrepancy that can be addressed within
the remaining time. State the chosen parameter family, expected visible change and anchors
that should remain stable in comments, then return one complete revised program. Avoid
rewriting unrelated builders. Compare the resulting full scene, not just the intended target.
Measured runtime/status, visual hypotheses and historical heuristics are separate evidence.
Controlled probe deltas, if supplied, are scope-limited sensitivity observations, not causal
proof or transferable camera/scale factors. Never invent measurements for missing outputs.
'''


def solve(task, runtime):
    # Read only the candidate's own instructions; no scene/data exploration.
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [
        {'role': 'system', 'content': prompt + '\n' + PROCEDURE},
        {'role': 'user', 'content': [
            {'type': 'text', 'text': (
                'Reconstruct this RGB image with editable SceneAct geometry. '
                f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. "
                'Return one complete economical program, including the evidence/ownership '
                'ledger and inline pre-execution self-review. Preserve major curved silhouettes, '
                'visible inventory and support; defer dense textile marks, floor subdivisions '
                'and tiny ornament. Plan camera using near and far anchors. '
                f'Available budget: {runtime.remaining()}')},
            runtime.image(task['image'])]}
    ]
    successes = []
    selected = None
    attempts = 0
    execution_history = []

    def note(text, image_path=None):
        content = [{'type': 'text', 'text': text}]
        if image_path:
            content.append(runtime.image(image_path))
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})

    def ask():
        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        messages.append(response)
        return _text(response).strip()

    def choose(text):
        match = re.fullmatch(r'SELECT\s+(\d+)', text, re.I)
        if match:
            step = int(match.group(1))
            if step in successes:
                return step
        return None

    while True:
        budget = runtime.remaining()
        if budget['seconds'] <= 0 or budget['model_calls'] <= 0:
            break
        if budget['executions'] <= 0:
            if successes:
                note('Execution budget exhausted. Review supplied successful full-scene renders '
                     'against the reference, including unchanged anchors, appearance and inventory. '
                     f'Successful steps: {successes}; current fallback: {selected}. '
                     'Reply SELECT <step> or DONE. Do not provide unexecutable edits or infer '
                     'success from a failed attempt. No unseen measurements are available.')
                text = ask()
                choice = choose(text)
                if choice is not None:
                    selected = choice
            break

        if execution_history:
            note('Scheduling evidence from this reconstruction only: ' +
                 json.dumps(execution_history, default=str) +
                 '\nElapsed values cover runtime.execute as observed through remaining seconds; '
                 'they do not isolate compilation, rendering or output time. A timeout does not '
                 'identify the slow phase. A camera-only revision still rebuilds the entire scene. '
                 'Allow time for a full execution and inspection; keep the successful fallback '
                 'if a useful revision is unlikely to finish. Do not alter fixed render settings. '
                 f'Remaining: {budget}')

        text = ask()
        choice = choose(text)
        if choice is not None:
            selected = choice
            break
        if text.upper() == 'DONE':
            if successes:
                break
            note('BUILD/OUTPUT FAILURE: no successful rendered output exists. DONE cannot '
                 'complete this reconstruction. Return a complete repaired economical program '
                 'if execution budget remains; do not invent a visual diagnosis.')
            continue
        blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S)
        if len(blocks) != 1:
            note('Return exactly one complete Python program in one python code block, '
                 'or DONE/SELECT <successful step> after a successful rendering. '
                 'Include your pre-execution self-review in program comments.')
            continue
        pending_code = blocks[0]

        # No extra model review or hidden execution: self-review is part of the draft.
        before = runtime.remaining()
        if before['executions'] <= 0 or before['seconds'] <= 0:
            break
        attempts += 1
        result = runtime.execute(pending_code)
        after = runtime.remaining()
        step = result.get('step')
        good = (result.get('ok') is True and bool(result.get('image'))
                and type(step) is int)
        elapsed = max(0.0, before['seconds'] - after['seconds'])
        execution_history.append({
            'attempt': attempts, 'step': step,
            'valid_render_returned': good,
            'execute_elapsed_seconds': round(elapsed, 3)
        })
        if good:
            successes.append(step)
            selected = step
        # Explicit fallback also protects against a later failed or missing output.
        if selected is not None:
            runtime.select(selected)

        feedback = {k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'}
        if not good:
            timeout = 'timeout' in str(result.get('error', '')).lower()
            instruction = (
                'BUILD/OUTPUT FAILURE: this attempt supplied no selectable successful render. '
                'Diagnose the reported error, missing image or missing step before visual work. '
                'There is no image-based evidence for this attempt. Preserve camera, semantic '
                'inventory, major dimensions and unrelated materials during repair. ')
            if timeout:
                instruction += (
                    'The reported timeout establishes a deadline failure, not its internal cause. '
                    'Review expanded primitive count, sweep sampling, repeated decorations, '
                    'unique finishes and costly Boolean assemblies. Reduce avoidable construction '
                    'cost while preserving important silhouettes, owners, supports and occluders. '
                    'Do not try to repair this by changing samples, engine or runner limits. ')
            else:
                instruction += (
                    'Review all related API assumptions, including primitive bounds, endpoints, '
                    'attachment names and world initialization. If no error explains missing '
                    'output, keep its cause unknown. ')
            instruction += 'Return a complete repaired program, or SELECT a prior successful step.'
        else:
            instruction = (
                'Compare this full-scene render with the reference. Separate measured facts from '
                'visual estimates and hypotheses. Check several near/far anchors, edge directions, '
                'foreground crops, whole-owner silhouette, support and occlusion. Also compare '
                'dominant light/dark regions and shadow contrast. Choose the largest supported '
                'discrepancy: camera, one semantic object with its dependents, or appearance. '
                'Hold other families fixed. An anchor unchanged across generated renders still '
                'needs comparison with the reference. Before moving an apparently absent object, '
                'check construction and all relevant wall faces and occluders; do not duplicate '
                'it or move it merely to expose it. Preserve local material non-target state and '
                'composite transforms. Return one complete revised program with a short prediction '
                'and inline self-review, DONE, or SELECT <successful step>.')
        note(json.dumps(feedback, default=str) + '\n' + instruction +
             f'\nAttempt: {attempts}. Successful steps: {successes}. '
             f'Current fallback: {selected}. Remaining: {after}',
             result.get('image') if good else None)

    if selected is not None:
        runtime.select(selected)

import json
from pathlib import Path
import re


def _text(response):
    content = response.get('content') or ''
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    if isinstance(content, list):
        return '\n'.join(item.get('text', '') for item in content if isinstance(item, dict))
    return ''


PROCEDURE = '''
Additional shared procedure:
Build reliability comes before visual refinement. The fixed execution budget includes
construction, realization and output, not just renderer sampling. Leave all runner-owned
render settings unchanged: do not set engine, samples, denoising, resolution, output paths
or time limits. Camera, lights, world and scoped materials remain available as documented.

Make the FIRST program a complete economical scene, with all major visible semantic
objects, their attached parts, support surfaces and foreground crops. Economize repeated
surface decoration before simplifying major silhouettes or deleting inventory. Use a small
reusable palette instead of assigning a distinct random finish to every repeated element.
A straight tube needs one rod; do not densely sample straight stretches. In this fixed
implementation sweep_tube with n points expands to 2*n-1 primitive meshes, and a fillet_box
can expand to 23 primitive meshes. Assemblies and clones do not merge their meshes.
Count expanded primitives, not builder calls. A few hundred primitive meshes is an initial
planning heuristic, not an acceptance threshold or a measured safe runtime. Spend extra
geometry on visible curvature and negative space only when its image contribution warrants it.
Do not alter SceneAct, merge semantic owners, or rename groups to influence target selection.

Include a concise self-review in the complete program's comments before submitting it:
- inventory, owner, actual support surface, camera anchors and uncertainty;
- repeated-loop expansion and the most expensive decorative families;
- positive finite dimensions, valid fillet radii, distinct rod endpoints, unique include slots;
- realize before camera/world/lights, and nonempty correctly scoped material descendants.
This is a code review, not an execution or a claim of passing regression tests. Do not spend
a separate model turn merely declaring READY unless explicitly asked for such a review.

Separate camera, each semantic object's dimensions/pose, and appearance parameters.
Before changing camera, compare at least three independent visible anchors spanning depth
and image position: architectural edge directions, a near crop/contact, and a far object or
wall feature. Record approximate reference/render locations and mismatch directions in
comments, marking them as visual estimates. An unchanged anchor between two generated
renders is not evidence that it matches the reference. Prefer a modest correction supported
by several anchors. Do not rescale all objects to compensate for an uncertain camera.
Review scale at the complete semantic owner, not an automatically selected trim or child.

Review broad appearance alongside geometry: light/dark region ordering, dominant surface
colors, shadow locations and contrast. A consistent appearance mismatch across several
surfaces may justify one lighting/world correction with geometry and camera fixed. A local
mismatch may justify a scoped material correction with lighting fixed. Do not change both
indiscriminately. Preserve non-target shaders, mesh data and transforms during local edits.
Never infer a material's physical color uniquely from its photographed brightness.

For missing objects, trace builder calls, attached returned nodes and unique include slots,
then compare camera projection, actual wall/support faces and nearer occluders. Construction
is not proof of visibility. Check the object's full extent against perpendicular wall returns
as well as its nominal support plane before translating it. Preserve reference occluders;
do not merely move an item until it is exposed. Keep all dependent parts together and
recompute actual contact offsets after size or pose changes. AABB tops and overlaps alone
cannot certify contact or collision. Unknown ownership/name mappings remain unknown.

After a valid render, choose the largest supported discrepancy that can be addressed within
the remaining time. State the chosen parameter family, expected visible change and anchors
that should remain stable in comments, then return one complete revised program. Avoid
rewriting unrelated builders. Compare the resulting full scene, not just the intended target.
Measured runtime/status, visual hypotheses and historical heuristics are separate evidence.
Controlled probe deltas, if supplied, are scope-limited sensitivity observations, not causal
proof or transferable camera/scale factors. Never invent measurements for missing outputs.
'''


def solve(task, runtime):
    # Read only the candidate's own instructions; no scene/data exploration.
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [
        {'role': 'system', 'content': prompt + '\n' + PROCEDURE},
        {'role': 'user', 'content': [
            {'type': 'text', 'text': (
                'Reconstruct this RGB image with editable SceneAct geometry. '
                f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. "
                'Return one complete economical program, including the evidence/ownership '
                'ledger and inline pre-execution self-review. Preserve major curved silhouettes, '
                'visible inventory and support; defer dense textile marks, floor subdivisions '
                'and tiny ornament. Plan camera using near and far anchors. '
                f'Available budget: {runtime.remaining()}')},
            runtime.image(task['image'])]}
    ]
    successes = []
    selected = None
    attempts = 0
    execution_history = []

    def note(text, image_path=None):
        content = [{'type': 'text', 'text': text}]
        if image_path:
            content.append(runtime.image(image_path))
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})

    def ask():
        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        messages.append(response)
        return _text(response).strip()

    def choose(text):
        match = re.fullmatch(r'SELECT\s+(\d+)', text, re.I)
        if match:
            step = int(match.group(1))
            if step in successes:
                return step
        return None

    while True:
        budget = runtime.remaining()
        if budget['seconds'] <= 0 or budget['model_calls'] <= 0:
            break
        if budget['executions'] <= 0:
            if successes:
                note('Execution budget exhausted. Review supplied successful full-scene renders '
                     'against the reference, including unchanged anchors, appearance and inventory. '
                     f'Successful steps: {successes}; current fallback: {selected}. '
                     'Reply SELECT <step> or DONE. Do not provide unexecutable edits or infer '
                     'success from a failed attempt. No unseen measurements are available.')
                text = ask()
                choice = choose(text)
                if choice is not None:
                    selected = choice
            break

        if execution_history:
            note('Scheduling evidence from this reconstruction only: ' +
                 json.dumps(execution_history, default=str) +
                 '\nElapsed values cover runtime.execute as observed through remaining seconds; '
                 'they do not isolate compilation, rendering or output time. A timeout does not '
                 'identify the slow phase. A camera-only revision still rebuilds the entire scene. '
                 'Allow time for a full execution and inspection; keep the successful fallback '
                 'if a useful revision is unlikely to finish. Do not alter fixed render settings. '
                 f'Remaining: {budget}')

        text = ask()
        choice = choose(text)
        if choice is not None:
            selected = choice
            break
        if text.upper() == 'DONE':
            if successes:
                break
            note('BUILD/OUTPUT FAILURE: no successful rendered output exists. DONE cannot '
                 'complete this reconstruction. Return a complete repaired economical program '
                 'if execution budget remains; do not invent a visual diagnosis.')
            continue
        blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S)
        if len(blocks) != 1:
            note('Return exactly one complete Python program in one python code block, '
                 'or DONE/SELECT <successful step> after a successful rendering. '
                 'Include your pre-execution self-review in program comments.')
            continue
        pending_code = blocks[0]

        # No extra model review or hidden execution: self-review is part of the draft.
        before = runtime.remaining()
        if before['executions'] <= 0 or before['seconds'] <= 0:
            break
        attempts += 1
        result = runtime.execute(pending_code)
        after = runtime.remaining()
        step = result.get('step')
        good = (result.get('ok') is True and bool(result.get('image'))
                and type(step) is int)
        elapsed = max(0.0, before['seconds'] - after['seconds'])
        execution_history.append({
            'attempt': attempts, 'step': step,
            'valid_render_returned': good,
            'execute_elapsed_seconds': round(elapsed, 3)
        })
        if good:
            successes.append(step)
            selected = step
        # Explicit fallback also protects against a later failed or missing output.
        if selected is not None:
            runtime.select(selected)

        feedback = {k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'}
        if not good:
            timeout = 'timeout' in str(result.get('error', '')).lower()
            instruction = (
                'BUILD/OUTPUT FAILURE: this attempt supplied no selectable successful render. '
                'Diagnose the reported error, missing image or missing step before visual work. '
                'There is no image-based evidence for this attempt. Preserve camera, semantic '
                'inventory, major dimensions and unrelated materials during repair. ')
            if timeout:
                instruction += (
                    'The reported timeout establishes a deadline failure, not its internal cause. '
                    'Review expanded primitive count, sweep sampling, repeated decorations, '
                    'unique finishes and costly Boolean assemblies. Reduce avoidable construction '
                    'cost while preserving important silhouettes, owners, supports and occluders. '
                    'Do not try to repair this by changing samples, engine or runner limits. ')
            else:
                instruction += (
                    'Review all related API assumptions, including primitive bounds, endpoints, '
                    'attachment names and world initialization. If no error explains missing '
                    'output, keep its cause unknown. ')
            instruction += 'Return a complete repaired program, or SELECT a prior successful step.'
        else:
            instruction = (
                'Compare this full-scene render with the reference. Separate measured facts from '
                'visual estimates and hypotheses. Check several near/far anchors, edge directions, '
                'foreground crops, whole-owner silhouette, support and occlusion. Also compare '
                'dominant light/dark regions and shadow contrast. Choose the largest supported '
                'discrepancy: camera, one semantic object with its dependents, or appearance. '
                'Hold other families fixed. An anchor unchanged across generated renders still '
                'needs comparison with the reference. Before moving an apparently absent object, '
                'check construction and all relevant wall faces and occluders; do not duplicate '
                'it or move it merely to expose it. Preserve local material non-target state and '
                'composite transforms. Return one complete revised program with a short prediction '
                'and inline self-review, DONE, or SELECT <successful step>.')
        note(json.dumps(feedback, default=str) + '\n' + instruction +
             f'\nAttempt: {attempts}. Successful steps: {successes}. '
             f'Current fallback: {selected}. Remaining: {after}',
             result.get('image') if good else None)

    if selected is not None:
        runtime.select(selected)


SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/frozen/backend/harnesses/baseline/policy.py characters [0,1537) of 1537
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re


def solve(task, runtime):
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [{'role': 'system', 'content': prompt}, {'role': 'user', 'content': [
        {'type': 'text', 'text': 'Reconstruct this RGB image. Match its camera and framing. ' +
         f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. Budget: {runtime.remaining()}"},
        runtime.image(task['image'])]}]
    while runtime.remaining()['model_calls'] > 0 and runtime.remaining()['seconds'] > 0:
        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        messages.append(response)
        text = response['content'] or ''
        blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S)
        if not blocks:
            if text.strip().upper() == 'DONE': return
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': 'Return one complete program in a python code block, or DONE.'})
            continue
        if runtime.remaining()['executions'] <= 0: return
        result = runtime.execute(blocks[-1])
        feedback = {'type': 'text', 'text': json.dumps({k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'}) +
                    '\nCompare the rendered camera view with the original reference. ' +
                    f'Fix errors with a complete revised program, or finish with DONE. Remaining: {runtime.remaining()}'}
        content = [feedback]
        if result['image']: content.append(runtime.image(result['image']))
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})


SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/frozen/initial_meta/generic_rsi/meta.py characters [0,1069) of 1069
"""CLI/backend adaptation of the HyperAgents self-referential meta entry point."""
def plan(packet):
    # Keep the complete explicit archive available and use the common probe menu.
    scenes = packet['development']
    probes = []
    if scenes and scenes[0]['status'] == 'complete':
        probes = [{'scene_id': scenes[0]['scene_id'], 'kind': kind} for kind in ['camera_focal', 'object_scale']]
    return {'probes': probes, 'regression_guidance': True,
            'instruction': 'Improve the shared task program H and your meta-program M. Inspect the entire supplied development archive, sources, execution errors, renders, geometry, and regression measurements. Design a general change to the reconstruction process under the fixed objective and limits. You may change meta.py planning, probe selection, evidence prioritization, and the next research instruction. Preserve callable plan(packet). The next candidate uses the latest valid meta program; this is an exploration branch, not acceptance of its task program. Do not specialize to scene identifiers.'}


SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/frozen/initial_meta/scene_rsi_pilot/meta.py characters [0,2694) of 2694
"""scene_rsi_pilot M_initial: bounded intervention and dependency regression guidance."""
def plan(packet):
    scenes = packet['development']
    probes = []
    diagnoses = []
    for scene in scenes:
        if scene['status'] != 'complete':
            diagnoses.append({'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'object_id': 'unknown', 'code_location': 'unknown', 'observation': 'No valid output; consult execution log', 'observation_type': 'measured', 'hypothesis': 'unknown', 'probe': 'none', 'probe_result': 'not_run', 'planned_change': 'Investigate general runtime failure before visual diagnosis'})
            continue
        target = scene.get('target_object', 'unknown')
        # Paired one-factor interventions distinguish camera from geometry sensitivity.
        probes = [{'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'kind': kind} for kind in ['camera_focal', 'object_scale']]
        diagnoses.append({'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'object_id': target, 'code_location': 'unknown', 'observation': 'Reference/render disagreement; magnitude is measured by fixed visual metrics', 'observation_type': 'measured_metric_with_unresolved_visual_cause', 'hypothesis': 'Camera framing or object scale may contribute; this is a hypothesis, not attribution', 'probe': 'camera_focal and object_scale', 'probe_result': 'awaiting controlled intervention', 'planned_change': 'Use probe sensitivity and dependency regression evidence to choose a shared H change'})
        break
    failures = {key: sum(not row.get('functional', {}).get(key, False) for row in scenes) for key in ['structure_pass', 'material_pass', 'composite_pass']}
    priorities = sorted(failures, key=lambda key: (-failures[key], key))
    return {'probes': probes, 'diagnoses': diagnoses, 'regression_priorities': priorities, 'regression_guidance': True,
            'instruction': 'Improve shared H and executable M using the controlled probe results and fixed functional regression evidence. Cover camera/object scale confusion, support/spatial relations, and occlusion/omission as applicable. Keep measured facts separate from visual hypotheses and historical heuristics. Link scene_id, object_id, code_location, observation, hypothesis, probe, probe_result, planned_change; unknown mappings must remain unknown. Do not assert causality from a single score delta. Use target modules/dependencies and modification risks to prioritize object structure, local material non-target preservation and composite movement checks. All final objectives and acceptance remain fixed. Modify meta.py only when an executable strategy change is justified; a memory append alone is not meta improvement. Return a reusable H, never a per-scene patched P.'}

    return {'probes': [], 'regression_guidance': True,
            'instruction': packet['sceneevolve_teacher_template'] + '\nADAPTATION: This is one candidate in a sequential K=2 pilot. Return one general shared candidate, preserve all full development trajectories provided, and retain the best Selection-eligible H including H0. The meta program is fixed for this baseline. Identical diagnostic tools and two probe slots are available; this initial fixed teacher schedule does not request them.'}

SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/validation_correction_v2/candidates/r1/generic_rsi/C1/meta.py characters [0,4001) of 4001
"""Evidence-led planning using only the supplied development packet."""


def plan(packet):
    scenes = [s for s in packet.get('development', []) if isinstance(s, dict)]
    observations = packet.get('controlled_probes', []) or []
    used = packet.get('executed_probe_count', len(observations))
    if not isinstance(used, (int, float)):
        used = len(observations)
    available = max(0, 2 - max(int(used), len(observations)))
    seen = set()
    for observation in observations:
        if isinstance(observation, dict):
            seen.add((observation.get('scene_id'), observation.get('probe')))
    failures = 0
    successes = 0
    for scene in scenes:
        trajectory = scene.get('trajectory') or {}
        for event in trajectory.get('events', []):
            if event.get('event') == 'execution_result':
                if event.get('ok') is True:
                    successes += 1
                else:
                    failures += 1
    complete = [s for s in scenes if s.get('status') == 'complete'
                and (s.get('metrics') or {}).get('valid') is True]

    def score(scene):
        value = (scene.get('metrics') or {}).get('overall')
        return value if isinstance(value, (int, float)) else float('inf')

    complete.sort(key=score)
    probes = []
    # Execution starvation is directly observable; further renders cannot diagnose it.
    if failures < max(1, successes):
        for scene in complete:
            if len(probes) >= available:
                break
            sid = scene.get('scene_id')
            if not isinstance(sid, str):
                continue
            functional = scene.get('functional') or {}
            kind = 'isolate' if functional.get('functional_pass') is False else 'camera_focal'
            if (sid, kind) not in seen:
                probes.append({'scene_id': sid, 'kind': kind})
    instruction = (
        'Improve the general shared reconstruction procedure and callable plan(packet), using the '
        'entire supplied development archive, sources, execution traces, images, geometry summaries '
        'and prescribed edit regressions. Do not explore external files or specialize to scene IDs. '
        'First distinguish build failures, missing outputs, exhausted correction opportunities and '
        'visual mismatch. Count actual execution_result events; regression render counts describe '
        'a different operation and must not be substituted for reconstruction successes. '
        'Check whether API safeguards recover first-build success and permit useful visual revisions. '
        'Then assess framing, visible object coverage, silhouette, occlusion and broad tone before '
        'fine detail. Compare all available development cases, including regressions and failures. '
        'Interpret diagnostic interventions only at their actual target and pivot: a tiny component '
        'scale change cannot identify whole-object scale error, and one focal perturbation cannot '
        'establish an optimal camera or true geometry. Missing measurements remain unknown; missing '
        'required output is failure. Structural/edit passes certify only the prescribed operations, '
        'not semantic completeness, physical contact or metric 3D accuracy; AABBs are approximate. '
        'Do not optimize names to manipulate target selection. Preserve meaningful assemblies. '
        'Propose a falsifiable next change, state expected evidence and risks, and retain complete '
        'working replacement files with the fixed APIs, roles, objective, splits and budgets. '
        'Selection aggregates may inform retention only; do not request Selection images or labels. '
        'The latest valid meta program governs the next exploration branch independently of task '
        'program acceptance. No independent evaluation or rendering is permitted in the proposer session.'
    )
    return {'probes': probes, 'regression_guidance': True, 'instruction': instruction}


SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/validation_correction_v2/candidates/r1/generic_rsi/C1/policy.py characters [0,5578) of 5578
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path


def solve(task, runtime):
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [
        {'role': 'system', 'content': prompt},
        {'role': 'user', 'content': [
            {'type': 'text', 'text': (
                'Reconstruct this RGB image. Match camera, framing, occlusions and major color regions. '
                f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. "
                f'Budget: {runtime.remaining()}. '
                'First produce a complete economical scene with all major visible objects. '
                'In brief code comments record approximate normalized image landmarks, camera assumptions, '
                'and the principal uncertain proportions. Audit API constraints before returning code. '
                'Keep camera, object dimensions and lighting parameters easy to revise. '
                'Do not spend the first build on dense subpixel decoration.'
            )},
            runtime.image(task['image'])
        ]}
    ]
    successful = []
    calls = 0
    executions = 0
    while calls < 5:
        budget = runtime.remaining()
        if budget['model_calls'] <= 0 or budget['seconds'] <= 0:
            break
        can_build = executions < 3 and budget['executions'] > 0
        if not can_build and not successful:
            break
        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        calls += 1
        messages.append(response)
        text = response.get('content') or ''
        if not isinstance(text, str):
            text = ''
        selection = re.fullmatch(r'SELECT\s+(\d+)\s*', text.strip(), re.I)
        if selection:
            step = int(selection.group(1))
            if step in successful:
                runtime.select(step)
                return
        if text.strip().upper() == 'DONE' and successful:
            runtime.select(successful[-1])
            return
        blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S)
        if not can_build:
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': (
                f'No executions remain. Select an observed successful build: {successful}. '
                'Reply SELECT followed by its step number. Do not generate unexecutable revisions.'
            )})
            continue
        if len(blocks) != 1:
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': (
                'Return exactly one complete Python program in one python code block. '
                f'Alternatively SELECT an observed successful step from {successful}, or DONE if one exists. '
                'No successful output means reconstruction failure, not completion.'
            )})
            continue
        budget = runtime.remaining()
        if budget['executions'] <= 0 or budget['seconds'] <= 0:
            break
        result = runtime.execute(blocks[0])
        executions += 1
        visible = bool(result.get('ok') and result.get('image'))
        step = result.get('step')
        if visible and isinstance(step, int):
            successful.append(step)
        report = {k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'}
        remaining = runtime.remaining()
        can_retry = executions < 3 and remaining['executions'] > 0
        if not visible:
            guidance = (
                'BUILD/OUTPUT FAILURE: no successful rendered view is available for this attempt. '
                'Do not claim a visual comparison. Read the error and audit the whole program for '
                'related faults, especially fillet radii, missing World, repeated include keys, '
                'zero-length rods and operations performed before realize. '
                'Return a complete corrected program if executions remain; preserve the intended composition.'
            )
        elif can_retry:
            guidance = (
                'Compare this render with the original reference in the first user message. '
                'In brief comments identify the three largest visible discrepancies and the parameter '
                'changes intended to fix them. Check image borders, major object extents, floor/wall '
                'boundaries, edge slopes, foreground/background size ratios, occlusions and broad tones. '
                'If errors move coherently across the scene, correct camera orientation/framing; '
                'if localized, correct the relevant object or placement. Do not change focal length '
                'and object scale indiscriminately. Preserve already matching regions and editable groups. '
                'Return one complete revised program, or SELECT an existing successful step if further '
                'changes are unjustified. Add fine detail only after composition is credible.'
            )
        else:
            guidance = (
                'No executions remain. Compare the successful rendered builds already shown with the '
                'reference. Reply SELECT followed by the best successful step number. '
                'Prefer framing, silhouettes, visibility and broad colors over detail count. '
                'Do not return a revised program.'
            )
        content = [{'type': 'text', 'text': (
            json.dumps(report, default=str) + '\n' + guidance +
            f'\nSuccessful steps: {successful}. Remaining: {remaining}'
        )}]
        if visible:
            content.append(runtime.image(result['image']))
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})
    if successful:
        runtime.select(successful[-1])

SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/validation_correction_v2/candidates/r1/scene_rsi_pilot/C1/meta.py characters [0,2694) of 2694
"""scene_rsi_pilot M_initial: bounded intervention and dependency regression guidance."""
def plan(packet):
    scenes = packet['development']
    probes = []
    diagnoses = []
    for scene in scenes:
        if scene['status'] != 'complete':
            diagnoses.append({'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'object_id': 'unknown', 'code_location': 'unknown', 'observation': 'No valid output; consult execution log', 'observation_type': 'measured', 'hypothesis': 'unknown', 'probe': 'none', 'probe_result': 'not_run', 'planned_change': 'Investigate general runtime failure before visual diagnosis'})
            continue
        target = scene.get('target_object', 'unknown')
        # Paired one-factor interventions distinguish camera from geometry sensitivity.
        probes = [{'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'kind': kind} for kind in ['camera_focal', 'object_scale']]
        diagnoses.append({'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'object_id': target, 'code_location': 'unknown', 'observation': 'Reference/render disagreement; magnitude is measured by fixed visual metrics', 'observation_type': 'measured_metric_with_unresolved_visual_cause', 'hypothesis': 'Camera framing or object scale may contribute; this is a hypothesis, not attribution', 'probe': 'camera_focal and object_scale', 'probe_result': 'awaiting controlled intervention', 'planned_change': 'Use probe sensitivity and dependency regression evidence to choose a shared H change'})
        break
    failures = {key: sum(not row.get('functional', {}).get(key, False) for row in scenes) for key in ['structure_pass', 'material_pass', 'composite_pass']}
    priorities = sorted(failures, key=lambda key: (-failures[key], key))
    return {'probes': probes, 'diagnoses': diagnoses, 'regression_priorities': priorities, 'regression_guidance': True,
            'instruction': 'Improve shared H and executable M using the controlled probe results and fixed functional regression evidence. Cover camera/object scale confusion, support/spatial relations, and occlusion/omission as applicable. Keep measured facts separate from visual hypotheses and historical heuristics. Link scene_id, object_id, code_location, observation, hypothesis, probe, probe_result, planned_change; unknown mappings must remain unknown. Do not assert causality from a single score delta. Use target modules/dependencies and modification risks to prioritize object structure, local material non-target preservation and composite movement checks. All final objectives and acceptance remain fixed. Modify meta.py only when an executable strategy change is justified; a memory append alone is not meta improvement. Return a reusable H, never a per-scene patched P.'}


SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/validation_correction_v2/candidates/r1/scene_rsi_pilot/C1/policy.py characters [0,1537) of 1537
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re


def solve(task, runtime):
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [{'role': 'system', 'content': prompt}, {'role': 'user', 'content': [
        {'type': 'text', 'text': 'Reconstruct this RGB image. Match its camera and framing. ' +
         f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. Budget: {runtime.remaining()}"},
        runtime.image(task['image'])]}]
    while runtime.remaining()['model_calls'] > 0 and runtime.remaining()['seconds'] > 0:
        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        messages.append(response)
        text = response['content'] or ''
        blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S)
        if not blocks:
            if text.strip().upper() == 'DONE': return
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': 'Return one complete program in a python code block, or DONE.'})
            continue
        if runtime.remaining()['executions'] <= 0: return
        result = runtime.execute(blocks[-1])
        feedback = {'type': 'text', 'text': json.dumps({k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'}) +
                    '\nCompare the rendered camera view with the original reference. ' +
                    f'Fix errors with a complete revised program, or finish with DONE. Remaining: {runtime.remaining()}'}
        content = [feedback]
        if result['image']: content.append(runtime.image(result['image']))
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})

SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/validation_correction_v2/candidates/r1/sceneevolve_cli/C1/policy.py characters [0,9704) of 9704
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re


GUIDANCE = '''
Reconstruction procedure:
Before writing the program, give a short visible-image specification: the main object
silhouettes and their approximate normalized image bounds (origin top-left), major
wall/floor boundaries, perspective directions, foreground cropping, and occlusion order.
These are estimates from RGB, not measured calibration. Choose camera position, target
and lens together to explain those observations. Put camera and major dimensions in
clearly named parameters. Build all major visible objects in the first program; defer
subpixel ornament until framing and large color regions agree. Preserve real 3D meshes
and meaningful multipart Asset groups throughout revisions.

Implementation facts to check before every execution:
- fillet_box requires 0 < radius <= half the smallest size component, including thickness.
  For a thin object, use an appropriately small radius or a different valid construction.
- realize clears Blender, including the world. After realize, obtain bpy.context.scene;
  if its world is None, assign bpy.data.worlds.new('World') before accessing world fields.
  Set world.use_nodes=True before accessing its Background shader.
- include replaces an existing child with the same key. Give repeated parts unique keys
  within their parent, including every loop index, so earlier geometry is retained.
- Use keyword arguments for rod endpoints. Every dimension must be positive and finite;
  consecutive sweep points and rod endpoints must be distinct.
- Transform pivots default to the object's bounding-box center. Specify pivot explicitly
  when rotating an assembly about a different point.
- SceneAct include keys name parent markers; generated mesh names may remain Box or Rod.
  For material edits, traverse the intended marker's mesh descendants, or retain explicit
  references, instead of assuming its include key is the mesh name. Copy shared materials
  before changing only one object's appearance.
- Create camera, lights, world and Blender material customization after realize. Keep
  the supplied image aspect ratio. Do not change fixed runner limits or render settings
  to obtain extra renders. Never call rendering, saving, external tools or file inspection.

After a successful render, compare the reference and render explicitly. Identify at most
three high-impact discrepancies, stating the affected image region and the proposed
parameter change. Prioritize camera/framing, silhouette/occlusion, and broad color/light
regions before fine detail. A global displacement suggests camera aim/shift; wrong
perspective slopes suggest orientation; wrong near/far size ratios suggest camera
position and focal length; a local mismatch suggests object geometry or placement.
Avoid changing camera and many unrelated object dimensions without a stated reason.
Return a complete standalone program for each revision, never a patch.
Missing output is a failed attempt, not an image to critique. Fix the reported error and
check the entire program for related errors before retrying. Do not invent render metrics.

The harness may request a source audit before the first execution. This is a model-only
review, not permission to run code. When asked to choose among successful renders, reply
SELECT <step> using a supplied step number. Choose by reference agreement, not recency.
DONE accepts the latest successful build. A selection is only valid for a successful
execution with an available rendered image.
'''


def _text(response):
    content = response.get('content') or ''
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    if isinstance(content, list):
        return '\n'.join(item.get('text', '') for item in content
                         if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get('type') == 'text')
    return ''


def _program(text):
    blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S | re.I)
    return blocks[-1] if blocks else None


def solve(task, runtime):
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [
        {'role': 'system', 'content': prompt + '\n' + GUIDANCE},
        {'role': 'user', 'content': [
            {'type': 'text', 'text':
             'Reconstruct this RGB reference. Give the short image specification and '
             'one complete program. Match camera, framing and visible geometry. '
             f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. "
             f'Budget: {runtime.remaining()}'},
            runtime.image(task['image'])]}]
    successful = []
    audited = False
    executions = 0

    while True:
        budget = runtime.remaining()
        if budget['model_calls'] <= 0 or budget['seconds'] <= 0:
            break
        if budget['executions'] <= 0 and not successful:
            break
        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        messages.append(response)
        text = _text(response)
        selection = re.fullmatch(r'\s*SELECT\s+(\d+)\s*', text, re.I)
        if selection:
            step = int(selection.group(1))
            if step in successful:
                runtime.select(step)
                return
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                             f'Invalid selection. Successful steps: {successful}. '
                             'Return an eligible SELECT step or a complete program if budget permits.'})
            continue
        if text.strip().upper() == 'DONE':
            if successful:
                runtime.select(successful[-1])
                return
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                             'No successful rendered output exists. Return a complete corrected program.'})
            continue

        code = _program(text)
        if code is None:
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                             'Return one complete program in a python code block, '
                             'or SELECT an eligible successful step.'})
            continue
        budget = runtime.remaining()
        if budget['executions'] <= 0:
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                             f'No executions remain. No new code can be submitted. '
                             f'Choose among successful renders already shown: {successful}. '
                             'Reply only SELECT <step>.'})
            continue

        # Spend one spare model turn preventing failures that would consume renders.
        # Keep the proposed program and audit response intact in the conversation.
        if (not audited and executions == 0 and budget['model_calls'] >= 4
                and budget['seconds'] > 180):
            audited = True
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                'Before execution, audit the complete proposed source without running it. '
                'Check all fillet radii against thickness, world initialization after realize, '
                'duplicate include keys inside loops, API signatures, material targeting, '
                'finite dimensions and camera placement. Also check the proposed camera '
                'and large silhouettes against your RGB specification. Return the complete '
                'corrected program, retaining sound geometry and avoiding extra ornament. '
                'There has been no render yet; do not claim visual validation. '
                f'Remaining: {budget}'})
            continue

        if runtime.remaining()['seconds'] <= 0:
            break
        result = runtime.execute(code)
        executions += 1
        valid = bool(result.get('ok') and result.get('image')
                     and result.get('step') is not None)
        if valid:
            step = result['step']
            successful.append(step)
            runtime.select(step)
        elif successful:
            runtime.select(successful[-1])

        budget = runtime.remaining()
        details = json.dumps({k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'},
                             default=str)
        if valid and budget['executions'] > 0:
            instruction = (
                'Compare the reference with this actual render. State up to three '
                'largest discrepancies and return a complete revised program addressing '
                'them. Preserve already matching geometry. If further edits are unlikely '
                'to help, SELECT the best successful step. ')
        elif valid:
            instruction = (
                'No executions remain. Compare all successful renders in this conversation '
                'with the reference and reply only SELECT <step>. Do not write unused code. ')
        else:
            instruction = (
                'FAILED OUTPUT: this attempt has no confirmed successful rendered image. '
                'Do not infer its visual appearance. Inspect the complete error and source; '
                'fix the cause and related hazards in one complete revised program. ')
        content = [{'type': 'text', 'text': details + '\n' + instruction +
                    f'Successful steps: {successful}. Remaining: {budget}'}]
        if valid:
            content.extend([
                {'type': 'text', 'text': 'Original RGB reference:'},
                runtime.image(task['image']),
                {'type': 'text', 'text': f"Rendered step {result['step']}:"},
                runtime.image(result['image'])])
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})
        if budget['executions'] <= 0 and len(successful) <= 1:
            break

    if successful:
        runtime.select(successful[-1])

The compartment render has substantially different foreground occupancy, object placement, lighting and color balance. Both show visible grain. Each trajectory contains two failed builds followed by one successful render and DONE, so neither had a rendered correction cycle. SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/validation_correction_v2/candidates/r1/sceneevolve_cli/C2/policy.py characters [0,11643) of 11643
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re


GUIDANCE = '''
Reconstruct from the supplied RGB only. Use the fixed SceneAct API and meaningful
multipart Asset groups. No tools, file exploration, reference textures, projections,
additional views, rendering calls or saving calls. The runner renders and saves.
Do not change runner settings, resolution, sampling, backend or resource limits.

Before coding, state a compact visible-image specification: major silhouettes and
normalized bounds (top-left origin), architectural boundary endpoints, perspective
line directions, foreground cropping, and occlusion order. Distinguish observations
from uncertain depth assumptions. Name camera position, target, lens and major object
dimensions as parameters. Build all substantial visible objects on the first attempt.
Match the visible negative spaces as carefully as occupied regions: openings between
supports, under furniture, around foreground objects and through the room.

Keep construction economical. SceneAct sweep_tube emits a sphere at every point and
one cylinder per segment; fillet_box emits many separate meshes. Long decorative
loops and floor grids can create thousands of objects before rendering even begins.
Use boxes for flat parts, rods for straight parts, and short sweeps for curved outlines.
Restrict repeated geometry to visible regions. Use procedural material patterns for
fine surface markings, never the input image. Avoid modeling subpixel stitches, grain,
weave and hidden flooring as separate objects. Preserve object identities and editable
parts; do not merge the entire scene. Prefer a few hundred primitives where possible,
without omitting important objects. Simplify detail further when time is short.

Check source before returning every program:
- fillet_box requires 0 < radius <= half the smallest size component.
- rod endpoints are keyword arguments and must be distinct. Consecutive sweep points
  must be distinct. All dimensions must be positive and finite.
- include replaces a child with the same key; include every loop index in repeated keys.
- Transform pivots default to bounding-box centers; specify other pivots explicitly.
- realize clears Blender. Create camera, lights and material customization afterward.
  Obtain bpy.context.scene after realize. Create its world if None and enable use_nodes
  before accessing Background. Mesh names need not equal SceneAct include keys:
  target mesh descendants of the relevant marker and copy shared materials for local edits.
- Keep all visible parts of an object under its meaningful assembly through revisions.

After rendering, compare actual images, not the intended effect of code. Use a small
residual table: region or landmark, reference estimate, rendered estimate, discrepancy,
likely cause, and proposed parameter change. Estimates are visual, not measurements.
Check architecture and foreground together before changing the camera. Global position
errors suggest aim/shift; slopes suggest orientation; near/far ratios suggest camera
position and lens. Local errors suggest geometry or placement. Explain any coupled
camera and geometry change. Prioritize silhouette, occlusion, framing, large color
regions and illumination over ornament. Avoid simultaneously darkening every material
and reducing all lights: isolate the dominant cause of a brightness discrepancy.
Do not infer hidden objects or exact physical dimensions from category conventions.

A visual audit is a model-only comparison, not permission to execute anything.
When requested, begin with BEST <step>, choosing the best available successful render
against the reference. This records a fallback, not a claim about an unrendered revision.
Then provide the requested critique and/or one complete standalone Python program in
one python fenced block. Preserve sound geometry and change at most three coherent
parameter families. Never return a patch. SELECT <step> ends reconstruction with that
successful output. DONE accepts the latest successful output. Missing or failed output
is a failure, not an image to critique. Repair the reported cause and related source
hazards; never invent metrics or claim visual validation of an unrendered program.
'''


def _text(response):
    content = response.get('content') or ''
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    if isinstance(content, list):
        return '\n'.join(item.get('text', '') for item in content
                         if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get('type') == 'text')
    return ''


def _program(text):
    blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S | re.I)
    return blocks[-1] if blocks else None


def solve(task, runtime):
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [
        {'role': 'system', 'content': prompt + '\n' + GUIDANCE},
        {'role': 'user', 'content': [
            {'type': 'text', 'text':
             'Give the compact RGB image specification, then a complete economical '
             'SceneAct program. Perform the source checks within this response. '
             'Include all major visible objects, but defer fine ornament. '
             f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. "
             f'Remaining budget: {runtime.remaining()}'},
            runtime.image(task['image'])]}]
    successful = []
    incumbent = None
    audited = False
    awaiting_audit = False
    execution_cost = 90.0

    def choose(step):
        nonlocal incumbent
        incumbent = step
        runtime.select(step)

    while True:
        budget = runtime.remaining()
        if budget['model_calls'] <= 0 or budget['seconds'] <= 0:
            break
        if budget['executions'] <= 0 and not successful:
            break

        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        messages.append(response)
        text = _text(response)
        best = re.search(r'^\s*BEST\s+(\d+)\s*$', text, re.M | re.I)
        if best and int(best.group(1)) in successful:
            choose(int(best.group(1)))
        selection = re.fullmatch(r'\s*SELECT\s+(\d+)\s*', text, re.I)
        if selection:
            step = int(selection.group(1))
            if step in successful:
                choose(step)
                return
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                             f'Invalid selection. Eligible successful steps: {successful}.'})
            continue
        if text.strip().upper() == 'DONE':
            if successful:
                choose(successful[-1])
                return
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                             'No successful output exists. Return a complete program.'})
            continue

        code = _program(text)
        if awaiting_audit:
            awaiting_audit = False
            if code is None:
                messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                    'Implement the visual audit in one complete program. Keep matching '
                    'parts intact. Begin BEST <step> to identify your fallback. '
                    f'Remaining: {runtime.remaining()}'})
                continue
        if code is None:
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                             'Return a complete program in one python fenced block, '
                             f'or SELECT an eligible successful step: {successful}.'})
            continue

        budget = runtime.remaining()
        if budget['executions'] <= 0:
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content':
                             'No executions remain. Reply only SELECT <step> from '
                             f'{successful}. Do not produce unused code.'})
            continue
        if budget['seconds'] <= 0:
            break
        # A reviewed incumbent remains available if an attempted revision fails.
        before = budget['seconds']
        result = runtime.execute(code)
        budget = runtime.remaining()
        execution_cost = max(execution_cost, max(0.0, before-budget['seconds']))
        valid = bool(result.get('ok') and result.get('image')
                     and result.get('step') is not None)
        if valid:
            successful.append(result['step'])
            if incumbent is None:
                choose(result['step'])
            else:
                runtime.select(incumbent)
        elif incumbent is not None:
            runtime.select(incumbent)

        # Preserve complete responses, code and reported result text in context.
        details = json.dumps({k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'},
                             default=str)
        can_revise = (budget['executions'] > 0 and
                      (not successful or budget['seconds'] >
                       120.0 + 1.25 * execution_cost))
        if not valid:
            if not can_revise:
                instruction = (
                    'FAILED OUTPUT. No confirmed image exists for this attempt. '
                    'Choose among the successful images already shown; reply SELECT <step>. ')
            else:
                instruction = (
                    'FAILED OUTPUT. Do not critique an absent image. Inspect the full '
                    'reported error and source. Return a complete repaired program. '
                    'For a timeout, reduce repeated primitive counts and decorative '
                    'construction while retaining major silhouettes and editable groups. ')
        elif not can_revise:
            instruction = (
                'Finish with visual selection. Compare every successful render with '
                'the RGB reference, prioritizing framing, silhouettes, occlusions and '
                'broad appearance. Reply only SELECT <step>. No new code. ')
        elif (not audited and len(successful) == 1 and
              budget['model_calls'] >= 4 and
              budget['seconds'] > 300.0 + 2.5 * execution_cost):
            audited = True
            awaiting_audit = True
            instruction = (
                'Perform a dedicated visual audit before rewriting code. Begin BEST '
                '<step>. Give the residual table for at most three high-impact '
                'discrepancies, including negative spaces and architectural boundaries. '
                'Separate camera errors from local geometry and lighting errors. '
                'Specify concrete parameter changes and what should remain fixed. '
                'Do not return code on this turn. ')
        else:
            instruction = (
                'Begin BEST <step> by comparing the available successful outputs to '
                'the reference. Give at most three residuals and a complete revised '
                'program addressing them. Preserve matching regions and keep geometry '
                'economical. If no worthwhile correction remains, SELECT the best step. ')

        content = [{'type': 'text', 'text': details + '\n' + instruction +
                    f'Eligible steps: {successful}. Reviewed fallback: {incumbent}. '
                    f'Remaining: {budget}'}]
        if valid:
            content.extend([
                {'type': 'text', 'text': 'Original RGB reference:'},
                runtime.image(task['image']),
                {'type': 'text', 'text': f"Actual rendered step {result['step']}:"},
                runtime.image(result['image'])])
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})
        if not can_revise and len(successful) <= 1:
            break

    if incumbent is not None:
        runtime.select(incumbent)

"""CLI/backend adaptation of the HyperAgents self-referential meta entry point."""
def plan(packet):
    # Keep the complete explicit archive available and use the common probe menu.
    scenes = packet['development']
    probes = []
    if scenes and scenes[0]['status'] == 'complete':
        probes = [{'scene_id': scenes[0]['scene_id'], 'kind': kind} for kind in ['camera_focal', 'object_scale']]
    return {'probes': probes, 'regression_guidance': True,
            'instruction': 'Improve the shared task program H and your meta-program M. Inspect the entire supplied development archive, sources, execution errors, renders, geometry, and regression measurements. Design a general change to the reconstruction process under the fixed objective and limits. You may change meta.py planning, probe selection, evidence prioritization, and the next research instruction. Preserve callable plan(packet). The next candidate uses the latest valid meta program; this is an exploration branch, not acceptance of its task program. Do not specialize to scene identifiers.'}


SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/validation_correction_v2/candidates/r2/generic_rsi/C1/policy.py characters [0,1537) of 1537
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re


def solve(task, runtime):
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [{'role': 'system', 'content': prompt}, {'role': 'user', 'content': [
        {'type': 'text', 'text': 'Reconstruct this RGB image. Match its camera and framing. ' +
         f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. Budget: {runtime.remaining()}"},
        runtime.image(task['image'])]}]
    while runtime.remaining()['model_calls'] > 0 and runtime.remaining()['seconds'] > 0:
        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        messages.append(response)
        text = response['content'] or ''
        blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S)
        if not blocks:
            if text.strip().upper() == 'DONE': return
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': 'Return one complete program in a python code block, or DONE.'})
            continue
        if runtime.remaining()['executions'] <= 0: return
        result = runtime.execute(blocks[-1])
        feedback = {'type': 'text', 'text': json.dumps({k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'}) +
                    '\nCompare the rendered camera view with the original reference. ' +
                    f'Fix errors with a complete revised program, or finish with DONE. Remaining: {runtime.remaining()}'}
        content = [feedback]
        if result['image']: content.append(runtime.image(result['image']))
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})

SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/validation_correction_v2/candidates/r2/scene_rsi_pilot/C1/meta.py characters [0,2694) of 2694
"""scene_rsi_pilot M_initial: bounded intervention and dependency regression guidance."""
def plan(packet):
    scenes = packet['development']
    probes = []
    diagnoses = []
    for scene in scenes:
        if scene['status'] != 'complete':
            diagnoses.append({'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'object_id': 'unknown', 'code_location': 'unknown', 'observation': 'No valid output; consult execution log', 'observation_type': 'measured', 'hypothesis': 'unknown', 'probe': 'none', 'probe_result': 'not_run', 'planned_change': 'Investigate general runtime failure before visual diagnosis'})
            continue
        target = scene.get('target_object', 'unknown')
        # Paired one-factor interventions distinguish camera from geometry sensitivity.
        probes = [{'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'kind': kind} for kind in ['camera_focal', 'object_scale']]
        diagnoses.append({'scene_id': scene['scene_id'], 'object_id': target, 'code_location': 'unknown', 'observation': 'Reference/render disagreement; magnitude is measured by fixed visual metrics', 'observation_type': 'measured_metric_with_unresolved_visual_cause', 'hypothesis': 'Camera framing or object scale may contribute; this is a hypothesis, not attribution', 'probe': 'camera_focal and object_scale', 'probe_result': 'awaiting controlled intervention', 'planned_change': 'Use probe sensitivity and dependency regression evidence to choose a shared H change'})
        break
    failures = {key: sum(not row.get('functional', {}).get(key, False) for row in scenes) for key in ['structure_pass', 'material_pass', 'composite_pass']}
    priorities = sorted(failures, key=lambda key: (-failures[key], key))
    return {'probes': probes, 'diagnoses': diagnoses, 'regression_priorities': priorities, 'regression_guidance': True,
            'instruction': 'Improve shared H and executable M using the controlled probe results and fixed functional regression evidence. Cover camera/object scale confusion, support/spatial relations, and occlusion/omission as applicable. Keep measured facts separate from visual hypotheses and historical heuristics. Link scene_id, object_id, code_location, observation, hypothesis, probe, probe_result, planned_change; unknown mappings must remain unknown. Do not assert causality from a single score delta. Use target modules/dependencies and modification risks to prioritize object structure, local material non-target preservation and composite movement checks. All final objectives and acceptance remain fixed. Modify meta.py only when an executable strategy change is justified; a memory append alone is not meta improvement. Return a reusable H, never a per-scene patched P.'}


SOURCE B26 experiments/scene_rsi_pilot/validation_correction_v2/candidates/r2/scene_rsi_pilot/C1/policy.py characters [0,1537) of 1537
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re


def solve(task, runtime):
    prompt = (Path(__file__).parent / 'system.txt').read_text()
    messages = [{'role': 'system', 'content': prompt}, {'role': 'user', 'content': [
        {'type': 'text', 'text': 'Reconstruct this RGB image. Match its camera and framing. ' +
         f"Image dimensions: {task['width']} x {task['height']}. Budget: {runtime.remaining()}"},
        runtime.image(task['image'])]}]
    while runtime.remaining()['model_calls'] > 0 and runtime.remaining()['seconds'] > 0:
        response = runtime.ask(messages)
        messages.append(response)
        text = response['content'] or ''
        blocks = re.findall(r'```python\s*\n(.*?)```', text, re.S)
        if not blocks:
            if text.strip().upper() == 'DONE': return
            messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': 'Return one complete program in a python code block, or DONE.'})
            continue
        if runtime.remaining()['executions'] <= 0: return
        result = runtime.execute(blocks[-1])
        feedback = {'type': 'text', 'text': json.dumps({k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'image'}) +
                    '\nCompare the rendered camera view with the original reference. ' +
                    f'Fix errors with a complete revised program, or finish with DONE. Remaining: {runtime.remaining()}'}
        content = [feedback]
        if result['image']: content.append(runtime.image(result['image']))
        messages.append({'role': 'user', 'content': content})
